-\,i\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\,\Bigl\{-\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}+\,\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}

+\,\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}-\,\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}\Bigr\}\,\delta({\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}).

6
Kinetic equation for soft gluon excitations

The self-consistent equations (
5.8
), (
5.9
) and (
5.18
) determine, in principle, the time evolution of number densities of the hard particles
{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pti^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}
and soft plasmons
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}
. However, we introduce one more simplification: in
Eq. (
5.18
), we disregard the term with the time derivative as compared to the term containing the difference in the eigenfrequencies of wave packets and hard particle energies. Instead of equation (
5.18
), we have

I^{\,i\;i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\simeq\delta({\bf p}-{\bf p}_{1})\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})\,{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\vskip 4.26773pt

(6.1)

+\;\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\ \mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\,\Bigl\{-\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\,a_{2}}+\,\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\,a_{2}}

+\,\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\,a_{2}}-\,\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i_{1}i}\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a_{1}\,a_{2}}\Bigr\}\,\delta({\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}),

where now the resonance frequency difference is

\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\equiv\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}+\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}.

(6.2)

The first term on the right-hand side of (
6.1
), which corresponds to completely uncorrelated waves (Gaussian fluctuations) is the solution to the homogeneous equation for the fourth-order correlation function

I^{\,i\;i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
. The second term determines the deviation of the four-point correlator from the Gaussian approximation for a low nonlinearity level of interacting waves.

We substitute the first term from (
6.1
) into the right-hand side of Eq. (
5.9
) for
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}
. As a result we obtain

-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355pt\!\int\!d{\bf p}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\bigr)\Bigl\{\!\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{I}\!}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{I}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}\Bigr\}.

(6.3)

Further, we substitute the second term from (
6.1
) into the right-hand side of Eq. (
5.9
). Simple algebraic transformations, in view of the symmetry condition (
5.3
), lead us to

i\hskip 1.13791pt\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\prime})\!\int\!d{\bf p}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd{\bf p}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{1}\,\biggl(\bigl|\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}

(6.4)

\times\left\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\!-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}t^{\,e}\bigr)\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\right.\!\!-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}

-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\!\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\vskip 4.26773pt

-\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl|\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\!\left\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf p}_{1},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}}-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\bigr)\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\right.\!\!-\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}\bigr)\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{\!a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}

-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}\bigr)\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}\bigr)\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt\!\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\biggr).

We consider the equality

\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}}-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,=\,-\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}+\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}.

to be evident by virtue of the definition (
6.2
). Taking into account the obtained expressions (
6.3
) and (
6.4
), changing, where necessary, the dummy color summation indices and reducing the factor
\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\prime})
, we get the the following kinetic equation for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}
, instead of (
5.9
):

\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf p}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\bigr)\Bigl\{\!\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{I}\!}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{I}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}\Bigr\}

(6.5)

+\,i\hskip 1.13791pt\!\int\!d{\bf p}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd{\bf p}_{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{1}\,\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\,\bigl|\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{2}

\times\left\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\!-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}\bigr)\right.\!\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\Bigr]\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}

-\,\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!-\,\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\!\Bigr)\vskip 4.26773pt

-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}\bigr)\!\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\Bigr]\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}

-\,\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{d}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{2}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\Bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\!\biggr\}.

In contrast to our previous works
[
15
,
1
]
, where the plasmon number density matrix
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}
was chosen as the unit diagonal matrix in color space (as well as the matrix function
{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.56917pti}_{\bf p}\,
), the required difference

\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\!-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\;\big(\!\equiv 2\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\delta(\Delta\hskip 0.85355pt\omega_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}_{2},\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}})\bigr).

(6.6)

is literally not collected here. In the kinetic equation (
6.5
) we have nontrivial arrangements of color matrices in the fundamental
t^{\,a}
and the adjoint
T^{\hskip 0.85355pta}
representations, and also the matrix densities of the number of plasmons
{\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}
and hard particles
{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}
. It is necessary to calculate the available traces in advance.

7
Approximation of the effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pti\;i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}

Let us consider approximation of the effective subamplitudes
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}
and
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{S}})}
, Eqs. (
5.13
) and (
5.14
), in the limit

|{\bf p}|,\,|{\bf p}_{1}|\gg|{\bf k}_{1}|,\,|{\bf k}_{2}|.

(7.1)

As a preliminary step, by virtue of the momentum conservation law in (
4.2
), we rewrite the expressions (
5.13
) and (
5.14
) setting

{\bf p}_{1}={\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}\equiv{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}.

Then, for example, for the first effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}
we have

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=T^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}

(7.2)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,\Biggl[\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt+{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}\,{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p}}

\displaystyle+\,\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}-{\bf k}_{2}}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}-{\bf k}_{2}}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}_{{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}-{\bf k}_{2}}\,{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k},\,{\bf p}-{\bf k}_{2}}\Biggr]

\displaystyle-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt\Biggl[\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}\,{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k},\,{\bf p}}

\displaystyle-\,\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}+\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}\,{\mathcal{V}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}\Biggr].

In the limiting case (
7.1
) for the expressions in the denominators on the right-hand side of (
7.2
) we get

\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}}\simeq-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{2},\qquad\varepsilon_{{\bf p}+\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\varepsilon_{{\bf p}}\simeq{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{1}

etc. Here, we have denoted
{\bf v}=\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\varepsilon_{\bf p}/\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}
. In light of these expansions we finally find an approximate expression for the effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}
:

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=T^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\Biggl(\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{1}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{2}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}\,{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}.

(7.3)

\displaystyle-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt\Biggl[\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}\,{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}

\displaystyle-\,\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1})}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}\,{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}\Biggr].

Fig.
1
gives the diagrammatic interpretation of different terms in the effective amplitude

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
.

Figure 1:
The effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
for the elastic scattering process of plasmon off a hard color particle. The blob stands for HTL-resummation and the double line denotes the hard particle

The first graph represents a direct interaction of two plasmons with hard test particle induced by the amplitude
T^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal A})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
in the general expression (
7.3
). The second and third graphs describe the Compton scattering of soft boson excitations off a hard particle. In the effective amplitude (
7.3
) they correspond to the term with product of the elementary interaction vertices of soft boson excitations with the hard test color-charged particle, namely
{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}
and
{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}
. The remaining graph is connected with the interaction of hard particle with plasmon and of three plasmons among themselves generated by the amplitudes
{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}
and
{\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}
with intermediate ‘‘virtual’’ oscillation.

Similar reasoning for the second effective subamplitude

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal S})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
(
5.14
) lead us to the following expression:

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal S})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=T^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\cal S})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,\Biggl[\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{2}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{1}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}\,{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}

\displaystyle-\,\Biggl(

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{2}}\,+\,\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{1}}\Biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt{\Phi}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}\,{\Phi}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf p}}\Biggr].

In the limit (
7.1
) the terms with the product
\Phi^{\ast}\hskip 0.56917pt\Phi
exactly reduce each other and thus

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{S}})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\simeq 0

within the framework of these approximations, as already mentioned in the section
5
. The complete effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,i\;i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
, Eq. (
4.3
), in this approximation has the simple color structure
(
5.16
), which, in turn, allows us to write the effective fourth-order Hamiltonian, Eq. (
4.2
), describing the elastic scattering process of plasmon off a hard color particle as follows:

{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pta_{3}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\int\!|\hskip 0.99594pt\zeta_{\bf p}|^{\hskip 0.99594pt2}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\;\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{\;{\bf p},\,{\bf p},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{1}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\,\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{3}},

where
d\hskip 0.99594pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}
is a differential solid angle with respect to the velocity direction
{\bf v}
, and the classical (commuting) color charge
\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}
on the right-hand side is defined as

\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\equiv\theta^{\,\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 0.28436pt(t^{\,a})^{i\hskip 0.85355ptj}\hskip 0.28436pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j}.

(7.4)

8
Approximation of the kinetic equation (
6.5
). The first moment with respect to color

Let us now turn to the approximation of the original kinetic equation (
6.5
). In the second term, we perform integration over
d{\bf p}_{2}
, which gives us
{\bf p}_{2}={\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf p}_{1}
and consider the approximation
|{\bf p}_{1}|\gg|{\bf k}|,\,|{\bf k}_{1}|
. By using the definition of the color charge (
7.4
), for the trace in the first term on the right-hand side of (
6.5
) we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}t^{\,e}\bigr)={\mathfrak{n}}^{i\hskip 0.56917ptj}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\bigl(t^{\,e}\bigr)^{j\hskip 0.56917pti}=n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\ast\ \!\!j}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl(t^{\,e}\bigr)^{j\hskip 0.56917pti}=n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Here,
n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}
is an ordinary scalar function of the momentum
{\bf p}
of a hard particle. Then in the second contribution on the right-hand side (
6.5
) we have for the difference of traces

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}+\Delta{\bf k}}\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e\!},t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\cdot\Delta{\bf k}\Bigr)+\,\ldots,

where we have designated
\Delta{\bf k}\equiv{\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}
. In the abelian case the first term on the right-hand side here is equal to zero and it is necessary to take into account the next term of the expansion that is linear in
\Delta{\bf k}
. This takes place in the theory of weak wave turbulence for ordinary electron-ion plasma (see, for example,
[
18
]
). Thus in the leading (zero) order in
\Delta{\bf k}
for the non-Abelian case we have for the difference of traces:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}+\Delta{\bf k}}\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\simeq{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e\!},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)\equiv if^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.0ptf}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}.

(8.1)

Let us consider further the more complex trace

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}+\Delta{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\bigr)+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl(t^{\,d}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\cdot\Delta{\bf k}\,t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}_{1}}\Bigr)+\,\ldots

=\biggl\{n^{2}_{{\bf p}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791pt{n}^{2}_{{\bf p}_{1}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt|{\bf p}_{1}|}\,({\bf v}_{1}\cdot\Delta{\bf k})\,+\,\ldots\,\biggr\}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr].

Here, unlike (
8.1
), we cannot immediately present this expression in terms of the product of two commutative color charges
\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}
and
\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}
. Let us rewrite the kinetic equation (
6.5
) once more, leaving only zero order in
\Delta{\bf k}
and assuming that the effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}
depends
only
on the velocity
{\bf v}={\bf p}/|{\bf p}|
:

\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{{\bf k}}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\!\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

+\;i\,\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\,\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\times\left(\frac{\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right)\vskip 8.5359pt

(8.2)

-\;i\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]

\times\left(\frac{\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}-\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}-\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right),

where we have replaced the integration variable
{\bf p}_{1}
by
{\bf p}
and supposed

\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}\equiv\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v}).

Further, the resonance frequency difference (
6.2
) in the expression (
8.2
) is approximated as

\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}\simeq\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}).

Consider the following color decomposition of the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}
:

{\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}_{\bf k}=\delta^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt+\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}.

(8.3)

We take the trace of the left and right-hand sides of (
8.2
) with respect to color indices, i.e., we set
a=a^{\prime}
and sum over
a
. Using the explicit representation (
8.3
) and the formulae for the traces of the product of two and three color matrices in the adjoint representation from Appendix
C
, Eqs. (
C.3
) and (
C.4
), we easily find for the trace on the left-hand side and for the traces in the first and third summands on the right-hand side of (
8.2
)

{\rm tr}\,{\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}=(N^{2}_{c}-1)\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\equiv d_{A}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},\qquad{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},

{\rm tr}\bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,e}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigr]={\rm tr}\bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigr]

=\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}N_{c}\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\bigr)+\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

The trace in the second term in (
8.2
) has a slightly more complicated structure and requires the use of the formula for the trace of the product of four matrices (
C.5
). Here, after contracting with
\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.85355pte}
we finally have

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr).

In obtaining this expression we used the symmetry property (
C.8
). This allowed us to easily eliminate the term with the product
W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
. Taking into account the obtained expressions for the color traces, we can now write out the first moment about color for equation (
8.2
)

d_{A}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(8.4)

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\,\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\times\,(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}))\vskip 8.5359pt

-\,N_{c}\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\,\Bigl\{\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)+\frac{i}{2}\,f^{\,e\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\Bigr\}

\times\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr](2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

Here, we have taken into account the Sokhotsky formula (
6.6
). We note that the expectation value of the color charge enters the first and the second terms on the right-hand side in the colorless quadratic combination
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. Furthermore, the last term in braces in (
8.4
) contains the imaginary part proportional to the sum
\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)
. However, it is easy to see that this contribution vanishes. Indeed, let us introduce the notation

Z^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\equiv\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr].

(8.5)

The symmetry property with respect to color indices
d
and
e
follows from the structure of this expression

Z^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pte}=Z^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd},

(8.6)

whence it immediately follows

f^{\,e\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355ptZ^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pte}=0.

(8.7)

Let us consider the first term in braces in (
8.4
) containing the difference

\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)
. Here, we have the contraction of the form

\delta^{\,e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355ptZ^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pte}=\bigl[(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr].

(8.8)

To disentangle this expression, it is necessary to use the Fierz identity for the
t^{\,a}
matrices, Eq. (
B.3b
). In this case we have

(t^{\,e})^{j_{1}i_{2}}(t^{\,e})^{i_{1}j_{2}}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\,i_{1}i_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\,j_{1}j_{2}}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,(t^{\,e})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}i_{2}}(t^{\,e})^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}j_{2}}

and therefore instead of (
8.8
) we obtain at once

\delta^{\,e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.56917ptZ^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pte}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(8.9)

Here we have introduced a notation for the mean value of the commutative ‘‘colorless’’ charge

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigr\rangle.

We see that it is impossible in this case to reduce the expression (
8.8
) only to a quadratic combination of color charges
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. The square of the mean value of the colorless Grassmann charges combination
\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
inevitably appears. Substituting the expression (
8.9
) into (
8.4
) we find finally the kinetic equation for the colorless part of the plasmon number density
N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}
:

d_{A}\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

+\;\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 2.84544pt

\times\,(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}))\vskip 8.5359pt

-\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}\hskip 0.85355pt-\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\hskip 0.56917pt\biggr\}\vskip 2.84544pt

\hskip 2.27626pt\times\,(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

9
The second moment with respect to color

Let us return to our original equation (
8.2
). Now let us contract the left- and right-hand sides of this equation with the color matrix
\bigl(T^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr)^{a^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pta}
. As a result, we find

\frac{\partial\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\,\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

+\;i\,\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.1)

\times\left(\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right)\vskip 8.5359pt

-\;i\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]

\times\left(\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right).

We consider the trace on the left-hand side and the traces in the first term on the right-hand side of Eq. (
9.1
). With allowance made for the color decomposition (
8.3
), simple calculations give

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},

(9.2)

{\rm tr}\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355pts}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}+\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\,e\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},\quad{\rm tr}\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355pts}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}-\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\,e\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}.

The imaginary part in the last two expressions will turn to zero under contraction with the color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and as a result the expression in braces in the first term in (
9.1
) may be cast in the following way:

\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=2\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(9.3)

Let us further consider more nontrivial traces in the second term in (
9.1
). For the first trace, taking into account the decomposition (
8.3
), we find the starting expression for the subsequent analysis

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\\[6.45831pt]
&+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}.\end{split}

(9.4)

For the traces of three and four generators in the adjoint representation of
SU(N_{c})
we make use of the corresponding formulae (
C.4
) and (
C.5
) given in Appendix
C
. If we contract the expressions obtained in this way with
\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, as it takes place in the original equation (
9.1
), then we get, instead of (
9.4
),

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}

-\,\biggl\{\frac{1}{2}\,i\,f^{\,c\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptf}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl({\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,f}D^{\,s}D^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr)\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 1.13791ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}

+\,\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 1.13791ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.42271ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

For the third trace on the right-hand side of (
9.4
) we have used the symmetry property (
C.8
), by virtue of which it turns to zero. Further, from the formulae (
C.4
) for third-order traces we have
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\sim{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,f}D^{\,s}D^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\sim f^{\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}
and therefore the second term proportional the product
W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.13791ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
also turns to zero by virtue of its contraction with the product
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
symmetric on the color indices
f
and
c
. We end up here with

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}

(9.5)

+\,\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

We just need to determine the contribution with the trace of five generators. This can be done directly using the general formula (
C.11
). Here, however, we choose another somewhat simpler way, using the fact that this trace is contracted with the matrix
\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}
.

Let us rewrite the contraction as follows:

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\equiv\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917ptb}\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{b\hskip 0.56917pta}.

Further, we can write

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{b\hskip 0.56917pta}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

=\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}a}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917pta}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}b}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{\hskip 0.85355ptf},D^{\,c^{\prime}}\bigr\}^{b\hskip 0.56917pta}-d^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigr)^{b\hskip 0.56917pta}\Bigr]\Bigr).

Here, we have used the formula (
C.5
) for the fourth-order trace. Let us contract the obtained expression with
(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917ptb}
. Finally, we get

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(9.6)

=\bigl\{T^{c^{\prime}},T^{f}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{f},D^{c^{\prime}}\bigr\}\bigr)-\,d^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\lambda}\bigr)\Bigr],

where in the last term we can immediately put
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\lambda}\bigr)=\frac{1}{2}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}
. We write the fourth-order trace on the right-hand side of (
9.6
) using the representation (
C.6
) and as a result it is equal to

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{f},D^{c^{\prime}}\bigr\}\bigr)=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\bigl(2\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}}\!-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.56917ptf}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}}\!-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\prime}}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptf}\bigr)

+\,\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-8}{4\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\bigl(2\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\!-d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\!-d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigr)\,+\,\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\!+d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigr).

According to (
9.5
), the expression (
9.6
) must be contracted with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. For the first term on the right-hand side of (
9.6
) we have the trivial equality

\bigl\{T^{\,c^{\prime}}\!,T^{\,f}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=0.

The contraction with the remaining terms in (
9.6
) gives us

\biggl(\frac{1}{8}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-\frac{1}{8}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\biggr)d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptf\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)(2-2)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv 0.

Thus the coefficient before the product
W_{{\bf k}}W_{{\bf k}_{1}}
in (
9.5
) is exactly zero. We independently verify this rather unexpected result for the special case
N_{c}=3
by directly computing the trace of the product of five matrices
T^{\,a}
.

For the trace
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)
in the second term in (
9.1
) we get similar result. In the end, for the expression in parentheses in the second term in (
9.1
), taking into account Sokhotsky’s formula (
6.6
), we obtain finally

\bigl(T^{\,f}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.56917pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\left(\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right)

(9.7)

=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}N_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

Let us now consider the traces in the last contribution on the right-hand side of the original equation (
9.1
). Here in the last trace
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)
in the expression in parentheses, we see a certain asymmetry in the arrangement of the matrix
T^{\,s}
under the sign of the trace in comparison to the other similar traces. Therefore, as a first step, by taking into account the decomposition (
8.3
), we transform this trace as follows:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\bigr]\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)-\frac{1}{2}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}.

The last term here contains the antisymmetric structural constant
f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}
and so it can be discarded by virtue of the relation (
8.7
). Given this fact and using Sokhotsky’s formula (
6.6
), the last line in equation (
9.1
) can be rewritten as follows:

\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}

(9.8)

=i\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

Then, considering the color decomposition (
8.3
), we transform the second trace on the right-hand side (
9.8
) as follows:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\equiv{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\bigr]{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\sim{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\{T^{\,d},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}.

In the final step here, we have taken into account that in the equation (
9.1
) this trace is contracted with the factor
Z^{d\hskip 0.85355pte}
symmetric in indices
d
and
e
as defined by (
8.6
). The advantage of choosing a trace with this arrangement of the matrices
T^{\,d}
and
T^{\,e}
is the automatic symmetry of the fourth-order traces (see below) over the permutation of the indices
d
and
e
, as is the case for the factor
Z^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}
. Taking into account the relation above, the difference of traces on the right-hand side of (
9.8
) takes then the following form

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(9.9)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\{T^{\,d},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}},

where, in turn, taking into account the decomposition (
8.3
) and the formulae for the traces of the third and fourth orders (
C.4
) and (
C.5
), we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(9.10)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}

=\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 1.13791pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{\,e},D^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}-d^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\Bigr]\Bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr).

Here, the first (imaginary) term on the right-hand side containing the sum of the colorless part of the plasmon number density
N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}
turns to zero when contracted with the factor
Z^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}
. The second term when using a different representation of the fourth-order trace of the matrices
T^{\,a}
, Eq. (
C.10
), can be represented in a slightly different form, simpler for further transformations

-\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr)-\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{T^{\,e},T^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}-d^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\Bigr]\Bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr).

In view of all the expressions (
9.2
), (
9.3
), (
9.7
), (
9.8
), (
9.9
) and (
9.10
) obtained above, the kinetic equation (
9.1
) for the color part
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}
of the plasmon number density takes the following form:

N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.11)

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}))\vskip 2.84544pt

-\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]

\times\biggl[\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr)-\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{T^{\,e},T^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}-\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\Bigr]\Bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)

+\,\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\{T^{\,d},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr]\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

Just below we will show that the third term on the right-hand side of (
9.11
) containing the color structure
Z^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte}
, Eq. (
8.5
), cannot be reduced to a function only of the averaged classical colorless and color charges
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
for an arbitrary value
N_{c}
. In addition, there is evident asymmetry with respect to the functions
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}
and
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
.

We consider separately the terms in braces in the next to the last line in (
9.11
), when contracting them with
Z^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte}
.
For the first term, allowing for (
8.9
) we have

\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}Z^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte}=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl[\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr].

Then using the relation (
B.8
) from Appendix
B
, we find for the third term

\bigl\{T^{\,e},T^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.56917ptZ^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte}=\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}+\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-2\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(9.12)

In the end, for the last term, by virtue of the relation (
B.6b
), we have

d^{\,e\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}Z^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}d^{\,e\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Collecting all the calculations above, we finally obtain for the expression in braces in (
9.11
)

\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr)-\frac{1}{8}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{T^{\,e},T^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}-2\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\Bigr]\Bigr\}\vskip 2.84544pt

(9.13)

\times\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]\vskip 5.69046pt

=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl\{\biggl[\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)-\frac{1}{8}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr]\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}+\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-8}{8\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 2.84544pt
