-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}d^{\,e\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

We see that here there remains only one ‘‘twisted’’ term associated with the second color structure in round brackets (
9.11
), namely with

\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr).

It generally does not allow to reduce the expression (
9.13
) to a combination of the colorless
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and color
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
charges. This can be done only for the special case
N_{c}=3
. Here we can use the relation (
B.9
) for the summand in the last line (
9.13
), which gives us

\Bigl[(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=2\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

+\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 1.13791pt\biggl\{\frac{1}{9}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{3}\,\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\bigg\}+\frac{2}{3}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-2\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}d^{\,e\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Considering this relation for the given particular value of
N_{c}
we find instead of (
9.13
)

\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pts}\bigr)-\frac{1}{8}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{T^{\,e},T^{\,d}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}-2\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\Bigr]\Bigr)\vskip 2.84544pt

(9.14)

\times\,\bigl[(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.56917pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!j_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!j_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]\big|_{N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt=\hskip 0.85355pt3}\vskip 2.84544pt

=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{8}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}+\frac{3}{8}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{7}{4}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 2.84544pt

-\,\frac{3}{2}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}d^{\,e\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Let us substitute (
9.14
) and (
9.12
) into the right-hand side of the kinetic equation (
9.11
). Reducing the left- and right-hand sides by the factor
N_{c}=3
, we find here finally for this particular value

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}\hskip 0.85355pt+\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\frac{d\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}\,=2\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(9.15)

+\,\frac{3}{2}\,\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\times\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}))\vskip 2.84544pt

-\,\frac{1}{3}\,\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\int\!d\hskip 1.13791pt\Omega_{\hskip 0.99594pt{\bf v}}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\,\biggl[\biggl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl(\frac{1}{12}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}-\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{3}{2}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}d^{\,e\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr\}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\,\biggl\{\frac{1}{3}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{\!2}+\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.56917ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-2\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr]

\times\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

10
Equation for the averaged colorless charge
\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle

In this and next sections, we analyze the kinetic equation for the hard particle number density
{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pti}_{{\bf p}}
defined by (
5.8
) in the approximation
|{\bf p}|,|{\bf p}_{1}|\gg|{\bf k}_{1}|,\,|{\bf k}_{2}|
. Let us write out the original equation here once more

\delta({\bf p}-{\bf p}\!\ ^{\prime})\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pti}_{{\bf p}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}\ =-\hskip 0.85355pti\!\int\!d{\bf p}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}

\times\,\biggl\{\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pti^{\prime}\,i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{\,{\bf p}^{\prime},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\,I^{\,i\,i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf p}^{\prime}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})\hskip 0.85355pt-\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pti\,i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{\ {\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\,I^{\,i_{1}\,i^{\prime}\,a_{2}\,a_{1}}_{{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf p}^{\prime},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf p}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})\biggr\}.

As the fourth-order correlation function
I^{\,i\,i_{1}\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
we take the expression (
6.1
). Following the same line of the reasoning as in section
6
, in this case we arrive at the following matrix kinetic equation supplementing Eq. (
8.2
):

\frac{\partial\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pti}_{{\bf p}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigl\{\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}\hskip 0.85355pt-\,\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k},{\bf k}}\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pti}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr\}

(10.1)

+\,i\!\int\!d{\bf p}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr|^{2}(2\pi)^{3}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf p}-{\bf p}_{1}+{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})

\times\biggl\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}-\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

-\,\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pti}\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)\vskip 4.26773pt

-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{{\bf p},\,{\bf p}_{1},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}-\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

-\,\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}_{1}}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{i^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pti}\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}T^{\,e}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)\!\biggr\}.\vskip 4.26773pt

Let us consider an approximation of this equation. The first step is to integrate over
{\bf p}_{1}
in the second term on the right-hand side of (
10.1
). This gives us
{\bf p}_{1}={\bf p}+\Delta{\bf k}
, where
\Delta{\bf k}\equiv{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}
. We are interested in the approximation
|{\bf p}|\gg|{\bf k}_{1}|,\,|{\bf k}_{2}|
. We compute the trace of the left- and right-hand sides over color indices, i.e. we set
i=i^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}
and sum over
i
. Taking into account that

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt)={\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pti}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}=n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 1.13791pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

we find instead of (
10.1
)

n_{\bf p}\,\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\,\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\Bigr\}

(10.2)

+\,i\hskip 1.13791pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\left\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\right.\hskip 71.13188pt

\times\biggl({\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[t^{\,e},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\biggr)

\hskip 56.9055pt-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,

\times\biggl({\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[t^{\,e},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\biggr)\!\biggr\}.

Within the approximations used in this paper, we have assumed that the function
{n}_{{\bf p}}
is independent of time.

We analyze the first term on the right-hand side of Eq. (
10.2
). Considering the traces

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=(t^{\,d})^{i\hskip 1.13791pti^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}{\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.85355pti^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pti}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}=n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,d})^{i\hskip 1.13791pti^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 1.13791pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv n_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 1.13791ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(10.3)

and

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{l}_{\bf k},

it is not difficult to see that the integrand in the first term on the right-hand side of (
10.2
) can be represented in the following form:

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\,\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\Bigr\}\\[5.59721pt]
&=2\hskip 0.28436pti\hskip 0.56917ptn_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 1.13791pt\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.\end{split}

Let us proceed to analyze the traces in the second term on the right-hand side of (
10.2
). Given that

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[t^{\,e},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptn_{\bf p},

we trivially find

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[t^{\,e},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

=i\hskip 0.85355ptn_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}+\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)

+\,\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917pte}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\hskip 0.56917ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pt\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{c},D^{\rho}\bigr\}^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}-d^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\rho\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigl(D^{\lambda}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]\Bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\Bigr\}

=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Here, we have used the representation (
8.3
) for the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and the formulae for traces (
C.3
) – (
C.5
).

Let us consider the other trace in the second term in (
10.2
), which differ in color structure. By virtue of the decomposition (
8.3
) and the traces (
C.3
) and (
C.4
), it can be represented as follows:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\Bigr]

(10.4)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)+\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{2}}+W_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

We examine the contribution proportional to the unit color matrix
\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}
. Taking into account the relation (
B.5
), we have the following chain of transformations

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=\frac{1}{2}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)-\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(10.5)

=\frac{1}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{2}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\,\ast\hskip 1.13791pti_{1}}\hskip 1.13791pt\theta^{\hskip 1.13791pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\,\ast\hskip 1.13791ptj_{1}}\hskip 1.13791pt\theta^{\hskip 1.13791ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.

Further, for the color factor
\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}i_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}
in the second term in (
10.5
) we make use of identity (
B.4
). Considering this identity, we find instead of (
10.5
)

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=\frac{1}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{2}-\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr\}.

The term in (
10.4
) with the antisymmetric structure constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355ptc}
will give us zero contribution due to the symmetry of the trace
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)
with respect to the permutation of indices
d
and
e
. Thus we finally obtain for (
10.4
)

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\Bigr]

=\frac{1}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{2}-\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\!\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr\}\bigl(N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

Taking into account all the above calculations, Sokhotsky’s formula (
6.6
) and reducing the left and right-hand sides by the common multiplier
n_{\bf p}
, we find instead of (
10.2
) the following equation for the averaged colorless charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
:

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 1.13791ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}

(10.6)

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\!\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))\hskip 19.91684pt

-\,n_{\bf p}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle^{2}-\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\!\hskip 0.85355pt\biggr\}\bigl(N_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\vskip 2.84544pt

\times\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})).

Here, we have introduced the shorthand notation for the colorless quadratic combination of the averaged color charge

{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\equiv\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(10.7)

Let us analyze the right-hand side of the obtained equation (
10.6
). The amplitude modulus square
\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}
, due to the first property in (
5.15
), is an even function with respect to the permutation
{\bf k}_{1}\rightleftarrows{\bf k}_{2}
. The resonance condition

\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

is also even with respect to the same permutation. Thus, we can see that the last two terms in (
10.6
) have odd the functions
\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)
and
\bigl(N_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)
, and therefore they are equal to zero, which leaves us with

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v}).

Further, let us take into account that the remaining term on the right-hand side is actually related to the collisionless (Landau) damping of the wave oscillations. Therefore the expression
{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})
must contain a
\delta
-function which reflects the corresponding conservation laws for energy and momentum:

{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\sim\int\!\frac{d\vskip 11.38092pt\Omega_{{\bf v}^{\prime}}}{4\pi}\,w_{{\bf v}^{\prime}}({\bf v},{\bf k})\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}^{\prime}\cdot{\bf k}),

where the probability
w_{{\bf v}^{\prime}}({\bf v},{\bf k})
for the Landau damping process can be determined using explicit expressions for the scattering amplitude (
7.3
), the three-point amplitude
{\mathcal{V}}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
, Eq. (
A.1
), and the HTL-correction
\delta\hskip 0.71114pt\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2})
, Eq. (
A.6
). However, as is well known, the
linear
Landau damping is kinematically forbidden in a hot quark-gluon plasma and therefore, this term can be setting zero and thus finally we obtain

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=0,

i.e.,

\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle={const}.

(10.8)

11
Equation for the averaged color charge
\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle

We now turn our attention to the derivation of the equation of motion for the color charge
\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle
.
For this purpose, we contract the left and right-hand sides of (
10.1
) with the matrix
(t^{\,s})^{i\hskip 1.13791pti^{\prime}}
.
Taking into account the trace (
10.3
), we find in this case instead of (
10.1
)

n_{\bf p}\,\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)-\,\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\!\Bigr\}

(11.1)

+\,i\hskip 1.13791pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}

\times\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

-\;{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)\vskip 4.26773pt

-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf p},\,{\bf p},{\bf k}_{1},{\bf k}_{2}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\Bigl(\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.28436ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)\biggr\}.

Let us analyze the first term on the right-hand side of Eq. (
11.1
). Using the formula (
B.1
) for the first trace in this term we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436pti\hskip 0.85355pti^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\theta^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\ \!\!i}\hskip 1.13791pt\theta^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!i^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=\biggl\{\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355ptn_{\bf p}.

(11.2)

The second trace
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)
trivially follows from (
11.2
) by rearranging the indices
s\rightleftharpoons d
.

Further taking into account the already known equality

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{l}_{\bf k},

it is easy to see that the integrand in the first contribution to (
11.1
) can be represented in the following form:

\begin{split}&{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)-\,\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\!\Bigr\}\\[5.59721pt]
&=i\hskip 0.85355ptn_{\bf p}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+N_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.\end{split}

We proceed to the analysis of the traces in the second term on the right-hand side (
11.1
). Our first step is to consider the following expression

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(11.3)

=\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}+\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)

+\,\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917pte}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\hskip 0.56917ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pt\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{c},D^{\rho}\bigr\}^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}-d^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\lambda}\bigl(D^{\lambda}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]\Bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\Bigr\}.

Here, we have used the representation (
8.3
) for the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and the formulae for the traces (
C.3
) – (
C.5
). We examine the term in braces with the simplest color structure
\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}
. With allowance made for the relations (
B.2
), the difference of traces in the square brackets in this case will be equal to

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}t^{\,s}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=-\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=-\frac{1}{2}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Thus, the term with
\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}
takes the form

-\frac{1}{2}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}.

Next, we consider the term mixed in
W_{{\bf k}}
and
N_{{\bf k}}
, containing the antisymmetric structure constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}
.
In this case, it is more convenient to represent the difference of traces in the square brackets as follows:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}t^{\,s}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\equiv{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[t^{\,e},t^{\,s}\bigr]t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\bigl[t^{\,e},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(11.4)

=\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,s}D^{\,\kappa}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}-\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,\kappa}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,if^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Here, we used the equality (
11.2
). The contribution with the ‘‘colorless’’ charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
is reduced. If we contract this expression with
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}=-i\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{d\hskip 0.85355pte}
and employ the formulae for third-order traces (
C.4
), then we obtain

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}t^{\,s}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]f^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd}

=-\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}D^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

=\frac{1}{4}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

The next step is to contract the above expression with the color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, as is the case of the term in (
11.3
), mixed by the functions
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}
and
N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}
. Then, the contribution of this term takes the final form

-\frac{1}{8}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 1.13791pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr).

Let us consider the remaining term in (
11.3
), proportional to the product of
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}
. With the use of the trace difference (
11.4
), it can be represented in a somewhat cumbersome form:

\biggl\{\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,s}D^{\,\kappa}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}-\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,\kappa}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]+\frac{1}{2}\,if^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\lambda\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!\biggr\}

(11.5)

\times\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917pte}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\hskip 0.56917ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pt\rho}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{\,c},D^{\,\rho}\bigr\}^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}-d^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\lambda}\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.56917ptd}\Bigr]\Bigr)\hskip 0.85355ptn_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}

=\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl\{\Bigl(-\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,c}D^{\,\rho}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,\rho}D^{\,c}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\Bigr]+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{\,c},D^{\,\rho}\bigr\}\bigr)-\,d^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,\kappa}D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)

-\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl(\bigl\{T^{\,c},T^{\,\rho}\bigr\}^{s\hskip 0.56917pt\kappa}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{\,c},D^{\,\rho}\bigr\}\!\bigr)-\,d^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,\kappa}D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)\Bigr]\Bigr)\biggr\}n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}.

The expression in parentheses in the last line is exactly the same expression that we obtained in analyzing the fifth-order trace in section
9
, Eqs. (
9.5
) and (
9.6
). There, it was shown that this expression vanishes. Let us consider the expression in parentheses in the next-to-last line. We write out this expression once more, setting by virtue of (
C.4
)

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,\kappa}D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)=i\hskip 0.28436pt\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}}\right)\!f^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\kappa\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda},

then

-\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,c}D^{\,\rho}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,\rho}D^{\,c}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\Bigr]+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{\,c},D^{\,\rho}\bigr\}\bigr)-\,i\hskip 0.56917pt\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\right)\!f^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\kappa\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,c\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\lambda}\Bigr].

(11.6)

We calculate the fourth-order trace, using the representation (
C.7
). It takes the form

{\rm tr}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\{D^{\,c},D^{\,\rho}\bigr\}\bigr)=i\hskip 1.13791pt\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-12}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)f^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,c\hskip 0.85355pt\rho\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}.

According to (
11.5
), the expression (
11.6
) should be contracted with

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. As a result, we have

\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{\rho\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

The color structure in the square brackets is zero. It can be easily verified by rewriting it in the following form:

\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{\rho\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}+\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{s\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}\equiv\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s},D^{\,\rho}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]^{c\kappa}-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917pt\rho\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}

and making use of the second relation in (
C.2
) from the Appendix
C
. Thus, the contribution proportional to the product
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}
completely drops out of consideration. Collecting all the calculated expressions, instead of (
11.3
), we finally find

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

(11.7)

=-\frac{1}{2}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\frac{1}{8}\,n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 1.13791pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\kappa}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr).

We proceed now to the consideration of the other expression in the second term in (
11.1
), with a different color structure. This expression in view of the decomposition (
8.3
) and the traces (
C.3
) and (
C.4
), can be represented as follows

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\Bigr]

(11.8)

={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\,d\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)+\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\,d\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr].

As usual, the first step is to analyze the contribution proportional to the trivial color structure
\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}
. Taking into account the relations (
B.5
) and (
B.7
), we have the following chain of transformations:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)=\frac{1}{2}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)-\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)

(11.9)

=\frac{1}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,\biggl(\frac{4}{N_{c}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+2\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\,s\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)\biggr\}

=\frac{1}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-2}{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}}\right)\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,d^{\,s\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}.

Our next task is to consider the term in (
11.8
) with the antisymmetric structure constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355ptc}
. Here, we need the relation (
B.6a
). Then, by the use of (
10.3
) and (
11.2
), we find

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355ptc}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr){\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigr\}

=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\biggl(\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}.

By contracting the obtained expression with
\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and adding to (
11.9
), we finally obtain, instead of (
11.8
),

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\Bigr]

(11.10)

=\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt(n_{\bf p})^{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl[\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\!\hskip 0.85355pt\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-2}{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}}\right)\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,d^{\,s\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\bigl(N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+d^{\,s\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\biggr].

It remains for us to compute the remaining expressions with traces on the right side of equation (
11.1
), namely

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigr]{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)

and

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{n}}_{{\bf p}}\bigr)\Bigl[\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr].

The calculation of the former gives us the expression (
11.7
), while for the latter we have (
11.10
). Taking into account all the above calculations, using Sokhotsky’s formula (
6.6
), instead of (
11.1
), we get the following equation for the averaged color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
:

n_{\bf p}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=n_{\bf p}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+N_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}

(11.11)

+\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptn_{\bf p}\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\,d^{\,s\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)\Bigr\}

\times\,\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))\vskip 5.69046pt

\hskip 56.9055pt-\,\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt(n_{\bf p})^{2}\!\hskip 0.85355pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

\times\,\biggl[\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\!\hskip 0.85355pt\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-2}{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}}\right)\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,d^{\,s\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\bigl(N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\vskip 2.84544pt

-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+d^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\biggr].

By virtue of the same reasoning we used after equation (
10.6
) describing the time evolution of the colorless charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, we can discard the contributions on the right-hand side of (
11.11
) containing the differences
\bigl(W_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}-N_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)
and
\bigl(N_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)
in the integrands. In addition, we multiply the left and right-hand sides by
{\bf p}^{2}
and then integrate over
|{\bf p}|
with the normalization

\biggl(\int\!n_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)=1.

(11.12)

As a result, we are left with the following evolution equation, instead of (
11.11
):

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+N_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}\vskip-14.22636pt

(11.13)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\!\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.28436pt\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\hskip 0.56917pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

\times\,\biggl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+d^{\,s\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)

with the initial condition

\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle|_{t\hskip 0.56917pt=\hskip 0.56917ptt_{0}}={\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}_{\hskip 0.85355pt0},

where
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.85355pt0}
is some fixed (non-random) vector of color charge that a high-energy particle possessed at the initial moment of time
t_{0}
.

We are interested in the time dependence of the quadratic combination of the color charge
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)
, as it defined by the expression (
10.7
). By virtue of equation (
11.13
) we easily find

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.85355pt\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)+N_{c\,}{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)\Bigr\}

(11.14)

+\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\!\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))\vskip 5.69046pt

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\hskip 0.56917pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

\times\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\bigl({\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\bigr)^{\!2}-\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

Here, we have introduced the notation for the second colorless combination of the third order in the averaged color charge

{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)\equiv d^{\hskip 1.13791pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

To close equation (
11.14
) we also deduce an equation for the function
{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)
:

\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=3\hskip 0.85355pt\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)+N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{4}(t)\Bigr\}

(11.15)

+\,\frac{3}{2}\,\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}N_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))\vskip 5.69046pt

\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\frac{3}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\hskip 0.56917pt\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}))

\times\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}(t){\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}(t)-\frac{1}{2}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt4}(t)\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\biggr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr).

However, on the right-hand side of this equation, a colorless combination of higher fourth order

{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt4}(t)={\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}(t)

(11.16)

appears, where

{\mathfrak{q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}(t)\equiv d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

It is clear that an attempt to write the equation for
{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt4}(t)
will in turn lead to more complicated colorless structures. A coupled chain of equations can be truncated at the first two combinations
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)
and
{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)
for the particular Lie algebra
\mathfrak{su}(3_{c})
(except for the ‘‘trivial’’ case
\mathfrak{su}(2_{\hskip 0.56917ptc})
). By virtue of the second relation in (
C.13
), the following representation for (
11.16
) is valid:

{\mathfrak{q}}_{4}(t)=\frac{1}{3}\,\bigl({\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\bigr)^{\!2}.

This allows us to completely close the system of three equations for the colorless charge
\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, Eq. (
10.8
), and equations for the colorless combinations
{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)
and
{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)
, Eqs. (
11.14
) and (
11.15
), respectively.

The equations (
11.14
) and (
11.15
) are presented in the most general form, which makes them quite complicated. Let us simplify them. As a first step, we take into account that due to the absence of linear Landau damping, it is necessary to put

{\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}({\bf v})=0.

We have already discussed this at the end of the previous section. Next, by virtue of (
10.8
), the ‘‘colorless’’ charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
must be assumed to be a constant value. For the sake of simplicity, we set this constant to zero

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{Q}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv 0.

Thus, instead of the evolution equations (
11.14
) and (
11.15
), we now get

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=A(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)+B(t)\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl({\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\bigr)^{\!\hskip 0.85355pt2},\!\qquad{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)|_{t\hskip 0.56917pt=\hskip 0.56917ptt_{0}}={\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0},

(11.17)

\displaystyle\frac{d\hskip 1.13791pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=\frac{3}{2}\,\bigl\{\!\hskip 0.85355ptA(t)-B(t)\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t),\quad{\mathfrak{q}}_{3}(t)|_{t\hskip 0.56917pt=\hskip 0.56917ptt_{0}}={\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0},

(11.18)

where we have introduced the notations

\displaystyle A(t)\equiv N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})),

(11.19)

\displaystyle B(t)\equiv\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.28436pt\biggl(\int\!n^{2}_{\bf p}\,{\bf p}^{2}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt|{\bf p}|\biggr)\!\!\int\!d{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd{\bf k}_{2}\,\bigl|\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\,(2,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{A}})}_{{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}({\bf v})\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!+W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!-\omega^{l}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\!-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})).

The initial values
{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}
and
{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}
are defined as

{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}={\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{0}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{0},\qquad{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}=d^{\hskip 1.13791pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}_{0}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}_{0}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}_{0}.

The equation (
11.17
) is a special case of the Bernoulli equation and, therefore, we can immediately write out its solution
[
19
]

{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}(t)={\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}\,\frac{\exp\left\{\,\displaystyle\int^{t}_{t_{0}}\!\!A(\tau)\hskip 0.85355ptd\tau\right\}}{1-{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt2}^{\hskip 0.28436pt0}\displaystyle\int^{t}_{t_{0}}\!\!B(\tau)\hskip 0.85355pt\exp\left\{\,\displaystyle\int^{\tau}_{t_{0}}\!A(\tau^{\prime})\hskip 0.85355ptd\tau^{\prime}\right\}d\tau},

(11.20)

which is qualitatively different from the solution we obtained in
[
2
]
. The second colorless combination
{\mathfrak{q}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt3}(t)
is trivially determined from the second equation (
11.18
). For physical reasons, we consider that the plasmon number density
N^{\,l}_{\bf k}
is a positive function that, by virtue of the definitions (
11.19
), leads in turn to the inequality

A(t)\geq 0.

Because of this, the exponential function in the solution (
11.20
) is an increasing function in time. On the other hand, the color part
W^{\,l}_{\bf k}
of the plasmon number density is, in general, indefinite and, as a consequence, the function
B(t)
can be either positive or negative. However, the solution (
11.20
) may nevertheless remain a finite value which is physically more reasonable.

12
Conclusion

In this paper we have demonstrated in detail that the Hamiltonian formalism proposed in
[
1
]
to describe the nonlinear dynamics of
only soft
Fermi- and Bose-excitations contains much more information
about the medium under consideration than was originally assumed. It turned out to be also very suitable for describing another range of physical phenomena, namely the processes of the scattering of colorless plasmons off
hard
thermal (or external) color-charged particles moving in a high-temperature quark-gluon plasma. The methodology developed in this paper allowed us to somewhat justify and define more exactly the formalism we proposed within the framework of heuristic approach in
[
2
]
