. In particular, this is reflected in the appearance of new contributions to both the kinetic equation for color part of the plasmon number density and the evolution equation (
11.13
) for the mean value of the color charge
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. The appearance of a new contribution to (
11.13
) could drastically change the dynamics of the color charge evolution in contrast to the conclusion of the paper
[
2
]
.

In this paper we have restricted ourselves to the detailed consideration of only the simplest process of the interaction of soft and hard modes in a quark-gluon plasma: the elastic scattering of plasmon off hard particle occurring without change of statistics of soft and hard excitations. At least for the weakly-excited system corresponding to the level of thermal fluctuations, this process is dominant.

Appendix A
Effective three-plasmon vertices

In this appendix we present an explicit form of the effective three-plasmon vertex functions
{\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
and
{\mathcal{U}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
. They were obtained earlier in
[
15
]
when constructing the Hamiltonian formalism for soft Bose excitations in a hot gluon plasma. These vertices
read

{\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=\frac{1}{2^{3/4}}\,g\hskip 0.85355pt\left(\frac{{\rm Z}_{l}({\bf k})}{2\omega_{\bf k}^{l}}\right)^{\!\!1/2}\!\!\!\frac{\tilde{u}_{\mu}(k)}{\sqrt{\bar{u}^{2}(k)}}\prod\limits_{i=1}^{2}\left(\frac{{\rm Z}_{l}({\bf k}_{i})}{2\omega_{\bf k}^{l}}\right)^{\!\!1/2}\!\!\!\frac{\tilde{u}_{\mu_{i}}(k_{i})}{\sqrt{\bar{u}^{2}(k_{i})}}\,^{\ast}\Gamma^{\mu\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}(k,-k_{1},-k_{2})\Bigr|_{\rm\,on-shell}\hskip 11.38092pt

(A.1)

and

{\mathcal{U}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=\frac{1}{2^{3/4}}\,g\hskip 0.85355pt\left(\frac{{\rm Z}_{l}({\bf k})}{2\omega_{\bf k}^{l}}\right)^{\!\!1/2}\!\!\!\frac{\tilde{u}_{\mu}(k)}{\sqrt{\bar{u}^{2}(k)}}\prod\limits_{i=1}^{2}\left(\frac{{\rm Z}_{l}({\bf k}_{i})}{2\omega_{\bf k}^{l}}\right)^{\!\!1/2}\!\!\!\frac{\tilde{u}_{\mu_{i}}(k_{i})}{\sqrt{\bar{u}^{2}(k_{i})}}\,^{\ast}\Gamma^{\mu\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}(-k,-k_{1},-k_{2})\Bigr|_{\rm\,on-shell}.

(A.2)

Two four-vectors

\tilde{u}_{\mu}(k)=\frac{k^{2}}{(k\cdot u)}\ \!\Bigl(k_{\mu}-u_{\mu}(k\cdot u)\Bigr)\quad\mbox{and}\quad\bar{u}_{\mu}(k)=k^{2}u_{\mu}-k_{\mu}(k\cdot u)

(A.3)

are the projectors onto the longitudinal direction of wavevector
{\bf k}
, written in the Lorentz-covariant form in the Hamilton and Lorentz gauges, respectively. Here,
u^{\mu}
is the four-velocity of the medium, which in the rest system is
u^{\mu}=(1,0,0,0)
. The explicit form of the effective three-gluon vertex
\,{}^{\ast}\Gamma^{\mu\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}(k,k_{1},k_{2})
on the right-hand side of (
A.1
) and (
A.2
) is defined by formulae (
A.4
) – (
A.6
) below.

Effective three-gluon vertex in the hard thermal loop (HTL) approximation has the following form
[
20
,
21
,
22
]

\,{}^{\ast}\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2})\equiv\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2})+\delta\hskip 0.71114pt\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2}),

(A.4)

where the first term is bare three-gluon vertex

\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2})=g^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu}(k-k_{1})^{\rho}+g^{\nu\rho}(k_{1}-k_{2})^{\mu}+g^{\mu\rho}(k_{2}-k)^{\nu}

(A.5)

and the second one is the corresponding HTL-correction

\delta\hskip 0.71114pt\Gamma^{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu\rho}(k,k_{1},k_{2})=3\hskip 0.99594pt\omega^{2}_{\rm pl}\!\int\!\frac{d\hskip 0.99594pt\Omega}{4\pi}\,\frac{v^{\mu}\hskip 0.56917ptv^{\nu}v^{\rho}}{v\cdot k+i\hskip 0.71114pt\epsilon}\,\Biggl(\frac{\omega_{2}}{v\cdot k_{2}-i\epsilon}-\frac{\omega_{1}}{v\cdot k_{1}-i\epsilon}\Biggr),\quad\epsilon\rightarrow+\hskip 0.56917pt0.

(A.6)

Here
v^{\hskip 0.85355pt\mu}=(1,{\bf{\bf v}})
,
k^{\hskip 0.85355pt\mu}=(\omega,{\bf k})
is a gluon four-momentum with
k+k_{1}+k_{2}=0
,
d\hskip 0.99594pt\Omega
is a differential solid angle and
\omega_{\rm pl}^{2}=g^{2}(2N_{c}+N_{f})T^{2}/18
is plasma frequency squared.

Appendix B
Relations and traces for generators in the defining representation of the
SU(N_{c})
color group

Let
t^{\hskip 0.85355pta},\,a=1,\ldots,\,N_{c}^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}-1
be the
SU(N_{c})
generators in the fundamental representations, then

t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}=\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb}\mathbbm{1}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}+i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355ptt^{c}

(B.1)

and, as a consequence, one has

t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\mathbbm{1},\qquad t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}.

(B.2)

Further, the Fierz identities for the
t^{\,a}
matrices are

\displaystyle(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}-\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}},

(B.3a)

\displaystyle(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}-\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}}.

(B.3b)

A trivial consequence of the first relation is the useful identity

\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+2\hskip 1.13791pt(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}}.

(B.4)

Next, the other consequence of (
B.3a
) is the relation for the trace of the following form:

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(A\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a\!}B\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a}\bigr)=\frac{1}{2}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(A\bigr){\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(B\bigr)-\frac{1}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(AB\bigr).

(B.5)

In addition, if we consider the following representations for the structure constants

f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}=-2\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a\!},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,b}\bigr]\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,c}\bigr),\qquad d^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}=2\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl\{\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a\!},\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,b}\bigr\}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,c}\bigr),

then, from (
B.3a
) and (
B.4
), it also follows that

\displaystyle f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\frac{i}{2}\,\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}-\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\Bigr\},

(B.6a)

\displaystyle d^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\Bigr\}-\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,d^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}(t^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,c})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}}.

(B.6b)

In deriving the last identity, we have used the relation for the sum

\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}=\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\bigl[\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,a})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\bigr]+2\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 1.13791pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}(t^{\,b})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,c})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}},

(B.7)

which is a consequence of (
B.4
) and (
B.1
). A similar relation for the difference trivially follows from (
B.6a
). Further, a useful consequence is also the relation

\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}

=\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl[(t^{\,c})^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,c})^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr].

In section
9
we require a special consequence of the previous expression, namely

\bigl\{T^{\,a},\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pti}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\frac{1}{N_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}

(B.8)

+\,\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}\Bigl[(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]-\Bigl[(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr].

Finally, we can write down an additional identity for the special case
N_{c}=3
:

(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}

(B.9)

+\;\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 1.13791pt\biggl\{\frac{1}{9}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}-\frac{1}{3}\,(t^{\hskip 0.85355pte})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\!\bigg\}

+\,\frac{1}{3}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355ptb}\Bigl[(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]-2\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355ptb}d^{\,\lambda\hskip 0.56917pt\kappa\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}(t^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,\rho})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}}.

This relation can be easily obtained if we first rewrite the left-hand side as

(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta}\hskip 0.28436pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}},

and then for the color structure
\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptc}+\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pti\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)
we use the first relation in (
C.13
) from Appendix
C
below and further employ the identities (
B.3b
), (
B.6b
) and (
B.8
). When we contract (
B.9
) with
\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptb}
and consider (
C.1
), we reproduce the identity (
B.3b
) for
N_{c}=3
, as it should be. Unfortunately, the relation (
B.9
) is not valid for arbitrary
N_{c}
. Indeed, if we use the general relation (
C.9
) for the color structure

\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)
, then, taking into account (
B.4
) and (
B.7
), by virtue of the relation

\bigl\{D^{\,a},\hskip 0.85355ptD^{\,b}\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\hskip 0.85355pta})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}=\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-2}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt3}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}-\frac{4}{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}}\,(t^{\hskip 0.85355pte})^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\!\bigg\}

+\,\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-8}{2\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\hskip 0.85355pt\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}\Bigl[(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr]+\Bigl[(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}+(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\Bigr].

-\,\frac{4}{N_{c}}\,\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{c\hskip 0.85355ptd}d^{\,\lambda\hskip 0.56917pt\kappa\hskip 0.85355pt\rho}(t^{\,\kappa}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt(t^{\,\rho})^{j_{1}\hskip 0.28436ptj_{2}}-\,\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\Bigl[(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{i_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{j_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}+(t^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt)^{\hskip 0.85355ptj_{1}\hskip 0.85355pti_{2}}(t^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt)^{\hskip 0.85355pti_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptj_{2}}\Bigr]

we arrive only at the identity.

Appendix C
Traces for generators in the adjoint representation of the
SU(N_{c})
color group

In this Appendix, we have provided an explicit form for the traces of adjoint representation matrices, which we use throughout our work. An extensive list of various traces, relations and identities for color matrices in the adjoint representation can be found in
[
23
,
24
,
25
,
26
,
27
,
28
]
. Initial definitions of the matrices
T^{\,a}
and
D^{\,a}
are

\bigl(T^{\,a}\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\equiv-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc},\qquad\bigl(D^{\,a}\bigr)^{\hskip 0.28436ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\equiv d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc},

where
f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
and
d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
are the totally antisymmetric and symmetric structure constants for the
SU(N_{c})
group, respectively. These matrices are traceless, i.e.

{\rm tr}\,T^{\,a}=0,\qquad{\rm tr}\,D^{\,a}=0

(C.1)

and satisfy the following commutation relations

\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a},T^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr]=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c},\qquad\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a},D^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr]=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,c}.

(C.2)

For completeness, we also provide the commutator for the
D^{\,a}
matrices

\hskip 17.07182pt\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,a},D^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr]^{c\hskip 0.56917ptd}=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\bigr)^{cd}+\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptc}-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptd}\bigr).

The traces of two generators are given by

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb},\quad{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,b}\bigr)=\left(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-4}{N_{c}}\right)\delta^{\hskip 1.13791pta\hskip 0.85355ptb},\quad{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,b}\bigr)=0,

(C.3)

and for the traces of three generators, we have, in turn,

\begin{array}[]{ll}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\bigr)=\displaystyle\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc},\\[17.22217pt]
\vskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\bigr)=\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc},\end{array}\qquad\begin{array}[]{ll}\vskip 5.69046pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptD^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\bigr)=i\hskip 1.42271pt\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-4}{2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}}\right)f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc},\\[8.61108pt]
\vskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,a}\hskip 0.85355ptD^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptD^{\,c}\bigr)=\left(\displaystyle\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-12}{2\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}}\right)d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}.\end{array}

(C.4)

The traces of four generators are

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355ptb}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{D^{\hskip 0.85355pta},D^{\,c}\bigr\}^{b\hskip 0.85355ptd}-d^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptc\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\bigr)^{b\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigr],

(C.5)

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-4}{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}}\biggr)\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptb}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptd}\!-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptd}\bigr)+\,\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-8}{4\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)\bigl(d^{\,a\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,c\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\!-d^{\,a\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigr)

(C.6)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+\,\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,a\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte},

\displaystyle{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}D^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=i\hskip 1.13791pt\biggl(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-12}{4\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}}\biggr)f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,c\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}+\frac{i}{N_{c}}\,\bigl(f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}-f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,b\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}\bigr)\!+\frac{1}{4}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,a\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.28436ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte}.

(C.7)

The representation (
C.7
) is convenient because it clearly shows the symmetry of the first term on the right-hand side and the antisymmetry of the second and third terms with respect to the permutation of indices
c
and
d
. We employ this fact in the section
11
. Further, the trace (
C.5
) is written in such a way that makes its symmetry with respect to the permutation of indices
a
and
c
, as well as with respect to the indices
b
and
d
, immediately apparent, i.e.,

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr).

(C.8)

If we use the anticommutation relation

\bigl\{\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a},T^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.56917ptd}+\bigl\{\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,a},D^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.56917ptd}=\frac{4}{N_{c}}\,\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptb}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.56917ptd}+2\hskip 0.56917ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{cd}-\frac{2}{N_{c}}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.56917ptd}\bigr),

(C.9)

then the trace (
C.5
) can also be represented in a slightly different form

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}+\frac{1}{2}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355ptb}\bigr)-\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.56917pt\Bigl[\bigl\{T^{\,a},T^{\,c}\bigr\}^{b\hskip 0.85355ptd}-d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptc\lambda}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(D^{\,\lambda}\bigr)^{b\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigr].

(C.10)

The trace of five generators
T^{\,a}
can be presented as a linear combination of the traces of four generators
[
29
]
3
3

3

In the paper
[
29
]
in the formula (45) for the trace of five generators in one of the terms on the right-hand side, two indices are incorrectly placed.

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)

(C.11)

\begin{split}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,\Bigl\{&f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{3}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{5}\hskip 0.56917pta_{4}\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr),\\[3.01385pt]
+\,&f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{3}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)+f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.56917pta_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptb}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr\}.\end{split}

This expression is a consequence of the sign reversal property of permutation of matrices
T^{\,a}
under the trace sign in reverse order

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)=-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr),

which in turn is a trivial consequence of the identity

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,a_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{3}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a_{4}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a_{5}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)=-\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(t^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a_{1}},\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptt^{\,a_{2}},\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptt^{\,a_{3}},\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptt^{\,a_{4}},\bigl[\hskip 0.85355ptt^{\,a_{5}},t^{\,b}\bigr]\bigr]\bigr]\bigr]\bigr]\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr).

The second-order Casimiris are

T^{\,a}T^{\,a}=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptI,\qquad D^{\,a}D^{\,a}=\left(\frac{N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}-4}{N_{c}}\right)\!I,

where
I
is the
\bigl(N^{2}_{c}-1\bigr)\!\times\!\bigl(N^{2}_{c}-1\bigr)
unit matrix. Also it is useful the following formula

T^{\,a}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,a}=\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,b}.

(C.12)

In addition, there are two additional identities for the special case
N_{c}=3

[
24
,
28
]
, which we use in the text of this article:

\begin{split}&\bigl\{\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,a},T^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.56917ptd}=3\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{cd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptb}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}-\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd},\\[4.30554pt]
&\bigl\{\hskip 0.56917ptD^{\,a},D^{\,b}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\}^{c\hskip 0.56917ptd}=-\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\,a\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl(D^{\,e}\bigr)^{cd}+\frac{1}{3}\,\bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptb}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355ptd}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptd}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pta\hskip 0.85355ptc}\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptd}\bigr).\end{split}

(C.13)
